\maketitle
\noindent{\sloppy\color{red}[\textbf{Phase changed to $e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}$ on the author's instruction (2026-11-20); the author's file had $e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}$.} The author's file \texttt{Final\_terms\_author\_2026-11-20.tex} used $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{primed}-\text{unprimed})}$ uniformly (all 57 phases, including the recoil phases; inventory by script, Sec.~\ref{sec:F}); here every phase is $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{unprimed}-\text{primed})}$, the convention of \texttt{fixed\_files/Final\_terms\_fixed.tex} and of the paper draft, and every changed phase is printed in red. This is $k\to-k$ in every term: the parts even in $k$ are unchanged, the parts odd in $k$ (symmetrised three-$\rho$ parts) change sign; the terms below are, in this convention, the verified terms.
\textbf{This file:} Secs.~1--4 are the author's BFKL, JIMWLK, DGLAP and running-coupling sections, unchanged except for the phases. Sec.~5 is the author's statement on the finite terms, followed by: Sec.~\ref{sec:conv} conventions and special functions; Sec.~\ref{sec:A} check of every finite term of the author's file against the verified files (with the \NOTE{}s); Sec.~\ref{sec:B} every finite term written explicitly; Sec.~\ref{sec:C} the cancellations, in colours (legend at the start of that section); Sec.~\ref{sec:D} what remains, with every three-$\rho$ product symmetrised exactly, Group by Group and Row by Row; Sec.~\ref{sec:E} the same list averaged over the projectile, in colour-singlet form; Sec.~\ref{sec:F} numerical checks. \textbf{Differences from the verified terms found in the author's finite terms} (details in Sec.~\ref{sec:A}): (1) the last term of $N_{3b}$ in Group I Row III is not equivalent to the verified recoil form (verified form in red, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3}); (2) the remnant term of Group II Row IV is missing (added in red, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-II4rem}). Everything else is equivalent to the verified terms (how verified: Sec.~\ref{sec:A}). \textbf{Two prescriptions} (author's instruction of 2026-11-20): Group I Row III is given with principal values (standing-wave dressing, as in the author's file) and with $i\epsilon$ (out-state dressing, $\eta=+1$), which adds the finite term $\Delta N$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}; pure glue ($N_{f}=0$).]\par}


\section{BFKL contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log\Big[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\Big]\,\int_{x,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[ \notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(x')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(x')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(w-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,N_{c}\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{a}(x') -\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x') +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x') -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
In color singlet form, we get
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{1}{N^{2}_{c}-1}\,\log\Big[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\Big]\,\int_{x,y,z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y) \rangle\,\Bigg[ \notag\\ & \mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,x,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+K(w,w',z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w')\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(x)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w')\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(x)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
where $K(x,y,z) = \frac{(x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}$
\section{JIMWLK contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}}\,\int_{x,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\Bigg[
\notag \\ & \mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
&+2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(w')\,U^{hb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(x')\,U^{hb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(w)\,U^{hb}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(x)\,U^{hb}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(x)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(w-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(w')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(x')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(w'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(w)\,U^{hb}(w')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{el}(x)\,U^{hb}(w')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(w-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(w)\,U^{hl}(w')\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(w')\,U^{hl}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(x)\,U^{hl}(w')\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(w')\,U^{hl}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\Big[\notag\\
& -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
Color singlet form
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{1}{N^{2}_{c}-1}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}}\,\int_{x,y,z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y) \rangle\,\Bigg[\notag \\ & \mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(w,w',z)\Big[4 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
&-2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(w')U(z)\}-2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,w',z)\Big[-4 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
&+2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}+2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,y,z)\Big[4 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}-2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(y,w,z)\Big[-4 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(y)\}\notag\\
&+2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}+2{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(w',w',z)\Big[-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(w')\}+2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(w')U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w')\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(w')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w')\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(w,w,z)\Big[-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(w')\}+2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(y)\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(w)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(w)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,x,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(w')\}-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(y,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(y)\}-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(y)\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
where $\mathcal{K}(x,y,z) = \frac{(x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}$
\section{DGLAP contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm DGLAP}}{d^{3}k}= &-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\frac{g^{2}}{8\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{\xi^{2}}\,\widetilde{\mathcal P}_{gg}(\xi)\,\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}\frac{1}{\xi}}\Big[2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4\,\xi^{2}}\Big]\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}(x'-y')^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\left[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\end{align}
where:
$\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{gg}(\xi)$ is gluon-gluon splitting function.
$$\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{gg}(\xi) = 2\,N_{c}\left[\xi(1-\xi) + \frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_{+}} + \Big[\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\Big]_{+}\right]+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}\,\delta(1-\xi),$$
\section{Running coupling contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm RC}}{d^{3}k}= & +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\frac{g^{2}\beta_{0}}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}}\Big[2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\Big]\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}(x'-y')^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\left[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\end{align}
We take $\frac{g^{2}\beta_{0}}{16\pi^{2}}=\frac{\alpha_{s}\beta_{0}}{4\pi}$ with $\alpha_{s}=\frac{g^{2}}{4\pi}$, and since $\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm RC}}{d^{3}k}$ has exactly the prefactor and the integrand of the LO cross section, the sum of LO and RC is
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm LO}}{d^{3}k}+\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm RC}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{2}(\mu)}{2\pi^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}}\left[1+\frac{\alpha_{s}(\mu)\,\beta_{0}}{4\pi}\log\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\,\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}\,e^{2\gamma_{E}}}{4}\right]\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\left[U^{ce'}(y')-U^{ce'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(y)-U^{ce}(x)\right]\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x).
\end{align}
\section{Finite terms}
Finite terms are obtained by $\Lambda \to 0$ and $\vee\to \infty $ and also by considering $d_{\perp} = 2-\epsilon$ and then we take $\epsilon \to 0$. 
\section{Conventions of this file}\label{sec:conv}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Phase.} ${\color{red}e^{-ik\cdot(\text{unprimed}-\text{primed})}}$ in all terms: $e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}$ (Groups I, II), $e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}$ with the recoil argument $s\equiv k\cdot(x-z)$ (Group I Row V), $e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}$ with $s\equiv k\cdot(z-y')$ (Group III Rows III, V, VI), $e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')}$ (LO, RC, Group II Row IV), $e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')/\xi}$ (DGLAP). Every phase that differs from the author's file is printed in red. Relative to the author's file this is $k\to-k$ in every term: the parts of the terms that are even in $k$ are unchanged, the parts that are odd in $k$ (the symmetrised three-$\rho$ parts, Secs.~\ref{sec:D}, \ref{sec:E}) change sign.
\item \textbf{Complex conjugate} implied in Groups I and III, not in Group II. The charges $\rho$ are operators in the order written. $\int_{x}\equiv\int d^{2}x$. $\mathcal{K}(x)\equiv\frac{x}{x^{2}}$ (vector), $K(x,y,z)=\frac{(x-z)\cdot(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}(y-z)^{2}}=\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)$, adjoint Wilson lines $U^{ab}$ (real, $U^{T}U=1$), $(T^{e})_{bc}=-if^{ebc}$, $(D^{e})_{bc}=d^{ebc}$.
\item \textbf{Limits.} $\Lambda\to0$, $\vee\to\infty$ after the logarithmic (BFKL, JIMWLK) terms are taken out, $\vee\to\infty$ before $\epsilon\to0$ (author's answer of 2026-11-20); terms $O(\Lambda/k^{+})$, $O(k^{+}/\vee)$ dropped. Energy denominators that vanish inside the integration region (only those of $\mathbb{B}_{3}$, which enter only Group I Row III and its complex conjugate) are given in \emph{both} prescriptions (author's instruction of 2026-11-20, ``Do it in two ways with $i\epsilon$ and PV''): principal value (standing-wave dressing of the final state; all equations unless stated otherwise) and $i\epsilon$ (out-state dressing, $\eta=+1$; $\eta=-1$ would be an in-state dressing of the final state, PV is the average), which adds the term $\Delta N$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}. \textbf{Pure glue}: $N_{f}=0$ throughout ($\beta_{0}=\frac{11N_{c}}{3}$).
\item \textbf{Plus prescriptions} act on the whole function to their right, including the phase and the energy denominators: with $t=p^{+}/k^{+}$,
\begin{align}\label{eq:plus}
\int_{0}^{1}dt\Big[\frac{1}{t}\Big]_{+}h(t)=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{dt}{t}\big[h(t)-h(0)\big]\,,\notag\\
\int_{0}^{1}dt\Big[\frac{1}{1-t}\Big]_{+}h(t)=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{dt}{1-t}\big[h(t)-h(1)\big]\,,\notag\\
\int_{0}^{\infty}dt\Big[\frac{1}{t(1+t)}\Big]_{+}h(t)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dt}{t(1+t)}\big[h(t)-h(0)\big].
\end{align}
\item \textbf{Functions of one real variable} (defined once here; ${\rm Ci}$, ${\rm Si}$, $E_{1}$ are the cosine, sine and exponential integrals):
\begin{align}
\mathcal{E}(s)&\equiv\int_{0}^{1}\frac{dt}{t}\big(e^{-ist}-1\big)={\rm Ci}(|s|)-\gamma_{E}-\log|s|-i\,{\rm Si}(s)\,,\label{eq:Ecal}\\
\mathcal{J}(s)&\equiv\int_{0}^{1}dt\,e^{-ist}=\frac{1-e^{-is}}{is}\,,\label{eq:Jcal}\\
\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)&\equiv\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dt}{1+t}\,e^{-ist}=e^{is}E_{1}(is)\,,\label{eq:Ehat}\\
\Psi(s)&\equiv\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dt}{t(1+t)}\big(e^{-ist}-1\big)=-\gamma_{E}-\log|s|-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(s)-\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\,,\label{eq:Psi}\\
\int_{0}^{\infty}dt\,e^{-ist}&=\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\qquad(\mathcal{P}:\ \text{principal value; complex conjugate }\pi\delta(s)+i\mathcal{P}\tfrac1s)\,.\label{eq:distr}
\end{align}
For $|s|\to\infty$: $\mathcal{E}(s)=-\gamma_{E}-\log|s|-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(s)-\frac{e^{-is}}{is}+O(s^{-2})$, $\Psi(s)=-\gamma_{E}-\log|s|-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(s)-\frac{1}{is}+O(s^{-2})$, $\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\simeq\frac{1}{is}$; for $s\to0$: $\mathcal{E}(s)\simeq-is$, $\Psi(s)=O(s\log|s|)$. All satisfy $f(-s)=f(s)^{*}$. $\mathcal{E}$ is the recoil function of Group I (soft integral up to $k^{+}$), $\Psi$ that of Group III (soft integral to infinity); at large $|s|$ both tend to the same function, including the odd part $-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(s)$.
\item \textbf{Integrable} means: integrable singularities at all coincidence points, and decay faster than $1/z^{2}$ at large position $z$ of a soft gluon (in practice $\log|z|/|z|^{3}$); a term $\propto e^{-ik\cdot z}/z^{2}$ (the measured momentum flowing through $z$) converges as a Fourier integral, as $\int_{w}e^{-ik\cdot w}\mathcal{K}(x-w)$ does at LO, and is not counted as a tail.
\end{itemize}

\section{Check of the author's finite terms against the verified versions}\label{sec:A}
\sloppy
\noindent Verified versions used: \texttt{fixed\_files/Final\_terms\_fixed.tex} (with \texttt{build/src}, \texttt{CHANGES\_fixed\_files.md}), \texttt{final\_checked/Final\_terms\_checked.tex}, \texttt{limits\_checked/Limiting\_cases\_final\_1\_checked.tex}, the paper draft \texttt{paper/draft/sec\_finite.tex}, \texttt{app\_finite.tex} and the fix notes \texttt{paper/notes/fix\_soft, fix\_poles\_dglap, fix\_singlet, fix\_tail, fix\_dabc.tex}. Every finite term of the author's file was compared, after $k\to-k$ (phase, Sec.~\ref{sec:conv}). Numerical checks: Sec.~\ref{sec:F}.

\paragraph{Phase.} The author's file has 57 $k$-dependent phases (BFKL 2, JIMWLK 2, DGLAP 1, RC 2, finite terms 50); all are $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{primed}-\text{unprimed})}$, including the recoil phases ($e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)p^{+}/k^{+}}$, $e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}e^{-ik\cdot\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}(y'-z)}$, $e^{-ik\cdot(y'-y)}e^{-ik\cdot(y'-y)p^{+}/k^{+}}$): the author's convention was uniform. On the author's instruction all are changed to $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{unprimed}-\text{primed})}$ (red). The internal Fourier phases $e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}$ of Group I Row II contain the loop momenta only and are unchanged.

\paragraph{Row by row.}
\begin{itemize}
\item[A1] \textbf{Group I Row III, combined kernel} (author's lines 1--4 of the row). Equivalent to the verified form. (i) The author's bracket $\big[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}^{i}(z-w)\mathcal{K}^{j}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}^{i}(x-w)\mathcal{K}^{j}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}^{i}(x-w)\mathcal{K}^{j}(x-z)\big]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}$ equals the logarithmic part of the verified kernel $\mathcal{M}^{ij}$ (paper Eq.~(Mij)) identically: with $a=x-w$, $b=x-z$, $c=z-w=a-b$, the difference of the two brackets times $(z-w)^{2}[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}]$ is $-c^{i}a^{j}-b^{i}c^{j}+c^{i}b^{j}+a^{i}c^{j}=c^{i}(b-a)^{j}+(a-b)^{i}c^{j}=0$. (ii) The arc-cosine argument $\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{|x-w|\,|z-w|}=\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)}{|x-w|\,|z-w|}$ is that of the verified $\mathcal{A}_{0}$; the bracket multiplying it is the verified one. (iii) The last line contains the verified last term of $\mathcal{M}^{ij}$ and the third term of $N_{3b}$, $-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,K(y,w,z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)$. Numerically: the author's lines 1--4 equal $\mathcal{K}^{i}(x'-w')\mathcal{K}^{j}(y-z)\mathcal{M}^{ij}+\ell\,K(y,w,z)\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)$ to $4\times10^{-13}$ (relative, 200 random points); $\mathcal{M}^{ij}$ equals the sum of the separate terms $N_{1},N_{2},N_{3a}$ and of the first two terms of $N_{3b}$ of the checked file to $5\times10^{-14}$ (\texttt{build/chk/kij.py}). Condition: the $w$ integral is done before the $z$ integral (Sec.~\ref{sec:C4}); principal values are implicit (the PV logarithms have combined into positive arguments).
\item[A2] {\color{red}\textbf{NOTE} (Group I Row III, last term of $N_{3b}$) --- \textbf{not equivalent}. Author: $-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\times[\dots U^{bc}(w)\dots]$, i.e.\ $+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int\ell(z,w,x)\,K(x,w,z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,[\dots]$ with $\ell=-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}$. With the verified term the tail cancels against Group III Row V (pair A; $z^{2}\times$sum $=6.7\times10^{-2},1.5\times10^{-2},1.4\times10^{-3}$ at $|z|=10^{2},10^{3},10^{4}$); with the author's term the two tails add ($z^{2}\times$sum $\simeq-32.6,-48.5,-64.2$, growing like $-2\log|z|$), and in addition the $z$ integral of the author's term at fixed $w$ has no $\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}$ partner. The BFKL, JIMWLK, DGLAP and RC sections were not re-checked (only their phases were changed); in the JIMWLK section the author writes ``where $\mathcal{K}(x,y,z)=\dots$'', meaning $K(x,y,z)$ of the BFKL section (left unchanged).

\section{Finite terms written explicitly}\label{sec:B}
\noindent Each term of the author's ``Three $\rho$ terms'' and ``Two $\rho$ terms'' lists is written out below in the convention of Sec.~\ref{sec:conv} (phase $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{unprimed}-\text{primed})}$, in red where it differs from the author's file). Where the author's term contains a plus prescription or a $p^{+}$ integral that can be done in closed form, the integral is done here and every piece is displayed: the terms with $\mathcal{E}$ and $\Psi$ are the \emph{soft remainders} of the plus prescriptions (they are the members of the pairs A--D of Sec.~\ref{sec:C}), the terms with $\widehat{\mathcal{E}}$, $\mathcal{J}$ and the $\xi$ integrals are the \emph{regular} terms, and the terms with $\pi\delta(s)-i\mathcal{P}\frac1s$ are the instantaneous terms. For every row it is stated whether it is the author's term unchanged (only the phase), the author's term with the integral done (equivalent, verified as stated in Sec.~\ref{sec:A}), or a corrected term (red). The complex conjugate is implied in Groups I and III.

\subsection*{Three $\rho$ terms}
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
The author's term, with the kernel written in its antisymmetrised (integrable) form; both colour structures are antisymmetric under $x\leftrightarrow y$, i.e.\ under $\mathbf{p}\to\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p}$, so this equals the author's form (Sec.~\ref{sec:A}, item A3):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{2b}}{d^{3}k} & = -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\quad\times\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{hbc}\,U^{ah}(w)\left\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\right\}-f^{abc}\,U^{bd}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\left\{\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\right\}\Big]\,,\label{eq:B-I2b}\\
R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p}) & = -2q^{i}\log\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{\mathbf{q}^{2}}\right)+\frac{(2\mathbf{p}^{2}-2\,\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{p}+\mathbf{q}^{2})\,q^{i}+\mathbf{q}^{2}p^{i}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\,\log\left[\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\right]\quad\text{(the author's $R^{i}$)}\,.\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
The author's combined kernel (lines 1--4 of the next equation, unchanged up to the phase) is verified to be the sum of the separate terms $N_{1}$, $N_{2}$, $N_{3a}$ and of the first three terms of $N_{3b}$ of the earlier version (\texttt{Final\_terms\_checked}); without the third term of $N_{3b}$ it is the combined kernel $\mathcal{M}^{ij}$ of the verified files, called the 24-term kernel below (Sec.~\ref{sec:A}, item A1). Its last line contains the \emph{third term of $N_{3b}$}, $-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)$ (it is the term $\frac{1}{2(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)$ of the author's last line). The \emph{last term of $N_{3b}$} is given in the verified form (red); the author's form of it is shown in orange directly after it and is \textbf{not} equivalent (\textbf{NOTE} A2). The $w$ integral has to be done before the $z$ integral (Sec.~\ref{sec:C4}).
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag \\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\times\, \log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag \\
&+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag \\
&+\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&{\color{red}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&{\color{red}\quad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)}\label{eq:B-I3}
\end{align}
{\color{orange}Author's last term of $N_{3b}$ (phase changed), not used: $-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\times\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]U^{de}(x)\big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)$.}
Here $\mathcal{E}(s)=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{dt}{t}(e^{-ist}-1)$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:Ecal}. The red term is the recoil form of the last term of $N_{3b}$: with $\lambda=\Lambda/k^{+}$, the last term of $N_{3b}$ together with its $\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}$ partner of $N_{3a}$ is, exactly in $\lambda$ (substitution of the soft-gluon position for the hard-gluon position under the Fourier integral),
\begin{align}
-\int_{w,z}e^{-ik\cdot w}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\Phi(w)\int_{\lambda}^{1-\lambda}\frac{dt}{t}\,e^{-it\,k\cdot(z-w)}=-\int_{w,z}e^{-ik\cdot w}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\Phi(w)\Big[\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}+\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot(z-w)\big)+O(\lambda)\Big],\notag
\end{align}
$\Phi(w)=\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,[U(x')-U(w')]^{ab'}\rho^{b'}(x')\,f^{abd}U^{de}(x)[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)$ (units $\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}$); the $\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}$ term is in the JIMWLK list, the $\mathcal{E}$ term is the finite term (paper draft, Eq.~(N3bexact)).

\paragraph{Row III in the two prescriptions (author's instruction of 2026-11-20: ``Do it in two ways with $i\epsilon$ and PV. We are in pure glue'').} The vanishing energy denominators $p^{+}(x-z)^{2}-k^{+}(x-w)^{2}$ of $\mathbb{B}_{3}$ (the only vanishing energy denominators of the calculation; they enter only this row and its complex conjugate) are taken (i) as principal values (standing-wave dressing of the observed gluon): Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3} as written; (ii) with $+i\eta0$, $\eta=+1$ (out-state dressing; $\eta=-1$: in-state dressing of the final state; PV is the average). With (ii) the $i\pi\theta$ pieces of the separate terms $N_{1},N_{2},N_{3a}$ combine exactly (paper notes, ref3\_prescriptions Eq.~(pr:shift)) into
\begin{align}
\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\;\longrightarrow\;\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-i\eta\pi\qquad\text{in the first two lines of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3} only}\notag
\end{align}
(the $\arccos$ term, $\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}$ and the last term are unchanged; the pole on $(x-z)^{2}=(x-w)^{2}$ cancels for every $\eta$). The bracket multiplying that logarithm is, exactly,
\begin{align}
&\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad=\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big[\cos2\phi\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)+\sin2\phi\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\times\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\,,\label{eq:B-Bij}\\
&\cos2\phi=\frac{[(x-w)\cdot(x-z)]^{2}-[(x-w)\times(x-z)]^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}\,,\qquad\sin2\phi=\frac{2\,[(x-w)\cdot(x-z)]\,[(x-w)\times(x-z)]}{(x-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}\,,\qquad a\times b\equiv a^{1}b^{2}-a^{2}b^{1}\,,\notag
\end{align}
$\phi$ the angle from $x-w$ to $x-z$. Row III with the $i\epsilon$ prescription is therefore Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3} plus
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\Bigg\{\int_{z}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abd}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\qquad-\int_{z}\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abd}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\qquad+\pi\Big[\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}{\color{gray}\;+\;\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}}\Big]\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abd}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\Bigg\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:B-DN}
\end{align}
($\eta=+1$; $\rho_{0}>0$ arbitrary, the result does not depend on it; prefactor $\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\times(-i\eta\pi)=\frac{\eta g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}$). The first term alone has a non-integrable $1/(z-w)^{2}$ at $z\to w$ (where $\phi\to0$ and the bracket tends to $\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2(z-w)^{2}}$); it is cut off by the window $|p^{+}-k^{+}|>\Lambda$ of $\mathbb{B}_{3}$, which gives $\int_{|z-w|<\rho_{0}}\frac{d^{2}z}{(z-w)^{2}}\big|_{\rm window}=2\pi\log\frac{\rho_{0}k^{+}}{\Lambda|x-w|}$; this is written as the subtraction in the second line plus the third line. {\color{gray}The term with $\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}$ (gray) is a forward Coulomb phase and cancels exactly against its complex conjugate (Sec.~\ref{sec:C7})}; what remains is finite and integrable. Properties (ref3\_prescriptions, a.2, a.7): $\int d^{3}k\,\frac{d^{3}\Delta N}{d^{3}k}=0$ (the on-shell part of $\mathbb{B}_{3}$ is anti-Hermitian; the total number of gluons is unchanged); $\Delta N$ is (real kernel)$\,+\,$c.c., while the principal-value terms of the row are $i\times$(real kernel)$\,+\,$c.c., so $\Delta N$ does not cancel against its complex conjugate and its parity in $k$ is the opposite of that of the principal-value terms: its symmetrised three-$\rho$ part is \emph{even} in $k$, its commutator (two-$\rho$) part \emph{odd} (Secs.~\ref{sec:D}, \ref{sec:E}). $\Delta N$ is linear in $\eta$. No other row has a vanishing energy denominator, so nothing else cancels $\Delta N$. Its size for a realistic configuration has not been evaluated.

\subsubsection*{Row IV}
The author's term (unchanged up to the phase); it cancels Group III Row I exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.IV}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{c'}(x)-f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{d}(x)\Big]\label{eq:B-I4}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
The author's term with the $p^{+}$ integrals done: with $t=p^{+}/k^{+}$, $\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{1}{k^{+2}}e^{-its}=\frac{1}{2\pi k^{+}}\mathcal{J}(s)$, $\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{1}{k^{+2}}\big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\big]_{+}e^{-its}=\frac{1}{2\pi k^{+}}\mathcal{E}(s)$ and, for the second term, $e^{-ik\cdot(x-w')}=e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}e^{-is}$, $\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dq^{+}}{2\pi}\big[\frac{1}{q^{+}}\big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)q^{+}/k^{+}}=\frac{1}{2\pi}\mathcal{E}(-s)$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Big[\mathcal{J}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{E}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)-e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\quad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\bigg[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(y')\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(y')\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(y')\}\bigg]\,,\qquad s\equiv k\cdot\ph{(x-z)}.\label{eq:B-I5}
\end{align}
The first term is regular, the second and third are the soft remainders of $\big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\big]_{+}$ and $\big[\frac{1}{q^{+}}\big]_{+}$ (soft gluon at $x$ and at $z$, respectively; pair B).
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I (first part)}
The author's terms (unchanged up to the phase); Group II has no complex conjugate:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{1a}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\Bigg]\times\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\,\notag \\
&\Bigg[U^{ac'}(w')\,\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{c'b}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,\{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\}\Bigg]\Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II1a}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{1b}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\Bigg]\times\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\right]\times\,\notag \\
&\Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{d'}(x)-f^{ac'd'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\Bigg]\Bigg[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{cb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\}\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II1b}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows II--IV} \textsc{NA}
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
The author's term (unchanged up to the phase); it cancels Group I Row IV exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.I}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\
& -\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Bigg]\times\, \log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\
& \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)-U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{c'}(x')\rho^{e'}(y)\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-III1}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
The author's term (unchanged up to the phase):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}} +\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\Bigg]\times\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\notag \\
&\Bigg[U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')\Bigg]\Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-III2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III (first part)}
With $t=p^{+}/k^{+}$ and $\vee\to\infty$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:A}, item A4): $\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}e^{-its}=\frac{1}{2\pi}\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)$, $\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\big]_{+}e^{-its}=\frac{1}{2\pi}\Psi(s)$, $\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{1}{k^{+2}}e^{-its}=\frac{1}{2\pi k^{+}}\big[\pi\delta(s)-i\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\big]$, Eqs.~\eqref{eq:Ehat}--\eqref{eq:distr}. The first line is regular, the second is the soft remainder of $\big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\big]_{+}$ (pair B), the third is the instantaneous term $-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ with its subtraction at $y'=z$; the two terms of the bracket of the third line belong to one $y'$ integral, done before the $p^{+}$ integral.
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\quad\times \Big[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{cb}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big],\qquad s\equiv k\cdot\ph{(z-y')}.\label{eq:B-III3one}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
As Row III (first part); the second line is the soft remainder of pair A.
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\quad\times U^{ce}(x)\Big[U^{ad}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y),\qquad s\equiv k\cdot\ph{(z-y')}.\label{eq:B-III5}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
As Row III (first part); the second line is the soft remainder of pair D. The colour structure is the author's (which equals the earlier form $[U^{c'e'}(y')U^{d'b}(z)-U^{d'b}(z)U^{c'e'}(x')]\rho^{e'}(x')[U^{cb}(z)U^{ad}(y)U^{ce}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\rho^{d}(y)\rho^{b}(x)]$ by $U^{d'b}(z)U^{cb}(z)=\delta^{d'c}$; checked at the operator level, Sec.~\ref{sec:F}).
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\quad\times U^{ad}(y)\Big[U^{d'e}(x)-U^{d'e}(z)\Big]\rho^{d}(y)\rho^{e}(x),\qquad s\equiv k\cdot\ph{(z-y')}.\label{eq:B-III6}
\end{align}
\subsection*{Two $\rho$ terms}
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
The author's term (unchanged up to the phase). The virtual $\frac{11}{3}$ terms of this row are not in the author's file because they cancel Row V exactly; they are displayed in Sec.~\ref{sec:C2}.
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\label{eq:B-I2a}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows III, IV} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
The author's two parts $N_{2a}$ and $N_{2b\,\rm rest}$ have the same kernel; their colour factors add up to $-f^{adc}f^{fbe}U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}U^{af}(y)$. With $\xi=p^{+}/k^{+}$, the plus prescriptions acting on the whole function $e^{-i\xi s}/[\xi(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)(y-z)^{2}]$ (Eq.~\eqref{eq:plus}), and $\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\,[\xi(1-\xi)-2]=-\frac{11}{6}$ for the last term of $N_{2b\,\rm rest}$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\,\frac{e^{-i\xi s}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&\qquad+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&\qquad-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad-\frac{\mathcal{E}(s)}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\frac{e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\,e^{-is}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\,,\label{eq:B-I5two}
\end{align}
$s\equiv k\cdot\ph{(x-z)}$. The $\xi$ integral (lines 1--7) is the regular term; lines 8 and 9 are the soft remainders of $\big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\big]_{+}$ (soft gluon at $x$) and of $\big[\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\big]_{+}$ (soft gluon at $z$), pair C; the last line is the collinear subtraction of $N_{2b\,\rm rest}$ ($e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}e^{-is}=e^{-ik\cdot(x-w')}$). In the notation of the paper draft, lines 1--7 are $\mathcal{K}^{i}(x'-w')\mathcal{K}^{m}(z-x)\mathcal{F}^{im}_{\rm reg}$ and lines 8, 9 are $-\mathcal{E}(s)\mathcal{K}^{i}\mathcal{K}^{m}B_{2}^{im}/(y-z)^{2}-e^{-is}\mathcal{E}(-s)\mathcal{K}^{i}\mathcal{K}^{m}B_{1}^{im}/(y-x)^{2}$, written out here.
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I (second part)}
The author's terms (unchanged up to the phase). They have to be kept together: on the surface $(x'-w')^{2}(x-z)^{2}=(x-w)^{2}(x'-z)^{2}$ each of $N_{2a}$, $N_{2b}$, $N_{2c}$, $N_{2d}$ has a simple pole; the poles cancel in $N_{2a}+N_{2b}$ and in $N_{2c}+N_{2d}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:A}, item A6).
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2a}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2b}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\qquad+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2c}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\qquad+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2d}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2e}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows II, III} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
The author's term (unchanged up to the phase; equal to the $\xi$ form $\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{8\pi^{4}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{k^{+}/\vee}^{1}d\xi\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\int e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')/\xi}[\dots]$ of the verified files by $\xi=k^{+}/(p^{+}+k^{+})$), and the remnant term of the verified files, which is missing in the author's file (red, \textbf{NOTE} A5):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\Big[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\label{eq:B-II4}\\
{\color{red}\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm rem}}{d^{3}k}}=&{\color{red}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[U^{ce'}(z)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)}\label{eq:B-II4rem}
\end{align}
The DGLAP and running-coupling terms of this row are those of Secs.~3 and 4 (the author's, phase changed). The remnant goes with the author's convention for the subtraction in the DGLAP term ($[\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}]_{+}$ subtracting $\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\times$ the value at $\xi\to0$); it is the $-1$ of $\int_{k^{+}/\vee}^{1}d\xi\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1$ (the logarithm is in the BFKL list).
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Rows I, II} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III (second part)}
The author's term with the plus prescription evaluated (the subtraction includes the phase and the energy denominator, Eq.~\eqref{eq:plus}); the coefficient of the last bracket of the first line group is $-\big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\big(1-\frac{p^{+}(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}(x-w)^{2}}{k^{+}(x-w)^{2}}\big)+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\big]=\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}$, and the subtraction at $p^{+}=0$ gives the $\Psi$ term (pair C). The last term is the author's (phase changed).
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,e^{-i\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}s}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,\frac{\Psi(s)}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,e^{-i\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}s}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag\\ &\qquad\times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right]\,,\label{eq:B-III3two}
\end{align}
$s\equiv k\cdot\ph{(z-y')}$; in the third term the $y'$ integral is done before the $p^{+}$ integral, and the two brackets stay together. The last term is what is left of the term added back for the subtraction in the third term after its $\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\log\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}$ part has been assigned to the BFKL list ($\int_{0}^{\infty}dp^{+}\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}(p^{+}+k^{+})[p^{+}(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}(x-w)^{2}]}=\frac{1}{k^{+}}\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}]}\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}$, the form of the author's last term).
\subsubsection*{Rows IV--VI} \textsc{NA}

\section{Cancellations}\label{sec:C}
\noindent\textbf{Colour legend.} Each cancelling set has its own colour; all members of a set are printed in the same colour.
\begin{center}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}\small
\begin{tabular}{|l|p{8.3cm}|p{5.2cm}|l|}\hline
colour & members & type & section\\\hline
{\color{cI4}\rule{2em}{1ex} green} & Group I Row IV $\leftrightarrow$ Group III Row I & \textbf{exact} (operator identity) & \ref{sec:C1}\\
{\color{cUV}\rule{2em}{1ex} teal} & Group I Row II $\frac{11}{3}$ term $\leftrightarrow$ Group I Row V $N_{2b\,\rm UV}$ ($-\frac{11}{6}$) & \textbf{exact} (pole, $2\gamma_{E}$, log) & \ref{sec:C2}\\
{\color{pA}\rule{2em}{1ex} orange} & pair A: Group I Row III (last term of $N_{3b}$) $\leftrightarrow$ Group III Row V ($\Psi$) & \textbf{tail only} & \ref{sec:C3}\\
{\color{pB}\rule{2em}{1ex} magenta} & pair B: Group I Row V first part ($\mathcal{E}$) $\leftrightarrow$ Group III Row III first part ($\Psi$) & \textbf{tail only} & \ref{sec:C3}\\
{\color{pC}\rule{2em}{1ex} violet} & pair C: Group I Row V second part ($\mathcal{E}$) $\leftrightarrow$ Group III Row III second part ($\Psi$) & \textbf{tail only} & \ref{sec:C3}\\
{\color{pD}\rule{2em}{1ex} brown} & pair D: Group I Row III (third term of $N_{3b}$, part $\mathcal{E}$) $\leftrightarrow$ Group III Row VI ($\Psi$) & \textbf{tail only} & \ref{sec:C3}\\
{\color{cPr}\rule{2em}{1ex} blue} & Group I Row III: third term of $N_{3b}$, part $\ell-\mathcal{E}$ $\leftrightarrow$ the 24-term kernel of the same row & \textbf{tail only, requires the $w$ integration first} & \ref{sec:C4}\\
{\color{cJt}\rule{2em}{1ex} olive} & Group II Row IV $\leftrightarrow$ Group II Row I (second part) $\leftrightarrow$ Group III Row III (second part), $1:1:-2$ & \textbf{tail only, requires joint integration} & \ref{sec:C6}\\
{\color{gray}\rule{2em}{1ex} gray} & $i\epsilon$ prescription only: the $\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}$ (Coulomb) term of $\Delta N$ (Group I Row III) $\leftrightarrow$ its complex conjugate & \textbf{exact} (operator identity) & \ref{sec:C7}\\\hline
\end{tabular}
\end{center}
``Exact'' means that the members are equal and opposite as integrands (after relabelling of integration variables), so that they can be removed. ``Tail only'' means that the members are different functions whose $1/z^{2}$ tails at large distance (each of which alone makes the integral divergent) are equal and opposite; the members are \emph{not} removed: what is left is an integrable remainder, which is part of the finite result and is written out below. Units: $\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}$ appears in all prefactors.

\subsection{Group I Row IV and Group III Row I: exact}\label{sec:C1}
\begingroup\color{cI4}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.IV}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{c'}(x)-f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{d}(x)\Big]\label{eq:C-I4}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.I}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\
& -\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Bigg]\times\, \log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\
& \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)-U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{c'}(x')\rho^{e'}(y)\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:C-III1}
\end{align}
\endgroup
\noindent\textbf{Cancels exactly} (both with c.c., same phase, same integration variables). The kernels are identical (the four terms in the brackets are the same, in a different order), the prefactors are $\pm\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}$, and the colour operators are equal: in Group III Row I, $[U^{ab'}(x')U^{c'd'}(y)U^{c'b}(z)-U^{d'b}(z)U^{d'e'}(y)U^{ac'}(w')\dots]=\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]U^{gb}(z)U^{gd'}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)$, so that
\begin{align}
&{\color{cI4}\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]U^{gb}(z)U^{gd'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]}\notag\\
&\quad{\color{cI4}=\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{c'}(x)-f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{d}(x)\Big]}\,,\notag
\end{align}
the colour operator of Group I Row IV (first term: relabel $b\to d$, $g\to b$, $d'\to e$, $d\to c'$; second term: $U^{gb}(z)U^{cb}(z)=\delta^{gc}$). This uses only relabelling of colour indices and $U^{T}U=1$; the order of the charges ($x'$, $y$, $x$) is the same, so it is an operator identity, and it holds also in colour-singlet form and for the symmetrised parts. (Numerically: colour tensors equal to $1.6\times10^{-15}$, $SU(3)$ and $SU(4)$, Sec.~\ref{sec:F}.) Neither row appears in Secs.~\ref{sec:D}, \ref{sec:E}.

\subsection{The $\frac{11}{3}$ ultraviolet terms of Group I Rows II and V: exact}\label{sec:C2}
These terms are not in the author's finite terms (they have been cancelled there); they are shown here explicitly (Divergent\_terms\_fixed, Eqs.~(dv-II), (dv-V); fix\_poles\_dglap Steps 1, 2, 7). Row II (one-$\rho$ part of $\mathbb{A}^{(3)}$, its $\frac{11}{3}$ part) and Row V ($N_{2b\,\rm UV}$, its $-\frac{11}{6}$ part), both with c.c.:
\begingroup\color{cUV}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{11/3}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{11}{3}\int_{w,w',x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\Big[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}-2\gamma_{E}-\log\frac{(x-w)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b}(x)\,,\label{eq:C-UV2}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2b\,\rm UV}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{-\frac{11}{6}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{11}{6}\Big)\int_{x,y,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-w')}}\Big[\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y-x)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\Big]\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\label{eq:C-UV5}\\
&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{11}{3}\int_{w,w',x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\Big[+\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(x-w)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b}(x)=-\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{11/3}}{d^{3}k}\,.\notag
\end{align}
\endgroup
\noindent The second form follows by renaming $x\to w$, $y\to x$: $\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{4}}(-\frac{11}{6})=-\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\frac{11}{3}$ and $[U(y)-U(x)]\to[U(x)-U(w)]=-[U(w)-U(x)]$, with the order $\rho(x')\rho(x)$ unchanged. \textbf{Cancels exactly}: pole, $2\gamma_{E}$ and logarithm, provided the same $\mu_{\overline{MS}}$ is used in both rows. Both poles are ultraviolet (UV against UV). Consequently there is no finite term $\frac{\alpha_{s}\beta_{0}}{4\pi}[\log(x-w)^{2}+\log(x'-w')^{2}-\log(w-w')^{2}]\times$LO. The remaining parts of the two rows are Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I2a} ($-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}$) and the $\frac{11}{6}$ collinear subtraction in Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I5two} (last line), which are finite.

\subsection{The soft remainders: pairs A--D (tail only)}\label{sec:C3}
Each Group I soft remainder (soft gluon absorbed by the observed gluon in the amplitude: virtual, recoil on $w$, function $\mathcal{E}$) has one Group III partner (soft gluon emitted by the observed gluon in the conjugate amplitude: real, recoil on $w'$, function $\Psi$) with the same coefficient, the same colour structure and the same kernel, except that $w$ is replaced by $w'$ in the kernel (fix\_soft Steps 2--4; paper Table~1). To see this, the Group III members are written with the integration variable $y'$ renamed $w'$, and the Group I Row V members with the row variables renamed (soft gluon at $z$, hard gluon at $w$). The colour identities used (operator identities: relabelling of colour indices, $U^{d'b}(z)U^{cb}(z)=\delta^{d'c}$, $f^{c'd'a}f^{dd'a}=N_{c}\delta^{c'd}$, no reordering of charges; checked numerically, Sec.~\ref{sec:F}) are
\begin{align}
&f^{c'ca}\Big[U^{c'e'}(w')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\Big[U^{ad}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\qquad=-f^{abd}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y),\notag\\
&f^{c'd'a}U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(w')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big[U^{ac}(w)\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{cb}(z)U^{ad}(y)U^{ce}(x)\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad=-\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big],\notag\\
&f^{c'd'a}U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(w')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big[f^{cbd}U^{ac}(w)\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}U^{cb}(z)U^{de}(x)\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad=-\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{ah}(x)-f^{adc}f^{hbe}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x),\notag\\
&f^{c'd'a}\Big[U^{c'e'}(w')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(x)\Big[U^{d'e}(y)-U^{d'e}(z)\Big]\rho^{d}(x)\rho^{e}(y)\notag\\
&\qquad=+f^{abd}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y).\label{eq:C-colid}
\end{align}
(the last one is the colour structure of Group III Row VI with $y'\to w'$ and $x\leftrightarrow y$). Each pair has the form $\kappa(w)\,\mathcal{E}(k\cdot(z-w))-\kappa(w')\,\Psi(k\cdot(z-w'))$ times a common factor, which is rewritten exactly as
\begin{align}
\kappa(w)\,\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot(z-w)\big)-\kappa(w')\,\Psi\big(k\cdot(z-w')\big)=\big[\kappa(w)-\kappa(w')\big]\,\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot(z-w)\big)+\kappa(w')\Big[\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot(z-w)\big)-\Psi\big(k\cdot(z-w')\big)\Big]\,.\label{eq:C-pairform}
\end{align}
At large $z$ each member behaves like $\pm\frac{T}{z^{2}}\big[-\log|k\cdot z|-\gamma_{E}-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(k\cdot z)\big]$ (not integrable); in the sum, $\kappa(w)-\kappa(w')=O(|w-w'|/|z|^{3})$ while $|\mathcal{E}|\le\log|z|+{\rm const}$, and $\mathcal{E}(k\cdot(z-w))-\Psi(k\cdot(z-w'))=\log\frac{|k\cdot(z-w')|}{|k\cdot(z-w)|}+O(1/|k\cdot z|)$ (the odd parts $-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}$ cancel), $O(1/|z|)$ outside the strip $|k\cdot z|\lesssim|k||w-w'|$ and bounded by logarithms inside it: \emph{the tails cancel, the remainder~\eqref{eq:C-pairform} is integrable}, for any $U(z)$ (the colour factor, with all its dependence on $U(z)$, is common to both terms). Numerically (fix\_soft Step 3), $z^{2}\times$(sum of the two members) falls like $1/|z|$ in every direction, for all four pairs, $SU(2)$ and $SU(3)$, two- and three-$\rho$ sectors. The complex-conjugate halves pair in the same way.

\paragraph{Pair A}
\begingroup\color{pA}
\begin{align}
&\text{Group I Row III, last term of $N_{3b}$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3}:}\notag\\
