&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\text{Group III Row V, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-III5}, $\Psi$ term:}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\Big[U^{ad}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\overset{y'\to w'}{=}+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y).\notag
\end{align}
\endgroup
Sum (the integrable remainder, Eq.~\eqref{eq:C-pairform} with $\kappa(u)=\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(u-z)$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm A}}{d^{3}k}&={\color{pA}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}\times\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\{\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big]\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}+\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big[\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)-\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\Big]\bigg\}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)}\label{eq:C-A}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Pair B}
\begingroup\color{pB}
\begin{align}
&\text{Group I Row V, first part, soft remainders, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I5} ($s=k\cdot(x-z)$):}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\Big[\notag\\
&\qquad\mathcal{E}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad+e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(y')\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(y')\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(y')\}\Big]\notag\\
&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\text{Group III Row III, first part, $\Psi$ term, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-III3one} ($s=k\cdot(z-y')$):}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{cb}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\overset{y'\to w'}{=}+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big].\notag
\end{align}
\endgroup
The Group I member is written in common variables by renaming, in its first term, $x\to z$ (soft), $z\to w$ (hard), $y'\to x$, and in its second term $x\to w$ (hard), $y\leftrightarrow y'$, then $y'\to x$ ($s\to k\cdot(z-w)$ in both, $e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}e^{-is}=e^{-ik\cdot(x-w')}$); the two terms are then equal (in the second both the kernel and the colour factor change sign). Sum:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm B}}{d^{3}k}&={\color{pB}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}\times\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\{\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big]\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}+\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big[\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)-\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\Big]\bigg\}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big]}\label{eq:C-B}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Pair C}
\begingroup\color{pC}
\begin{align}
&\text{Group I Row V, second part, soft remainders, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I5two} ($s=k\cdot(x-z)$):}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\bigg\{\notag\\
&\qquad\frac{\mathcal{E}(s)}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\notag\\
&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,z,x'}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)-\frac{2}{(z-w)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{ah}(x)-f^{adc}f^{hbe}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)\notag\\
&\text{Group III Row III, second part, $\Psi$ term, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-III3two} ($s=k\cdot(z-y')$):}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{\Psi(s)}{(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\overset{y'\to w'}{=}+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,z,x'}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-2\,\mathcal{K}(w-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{ah}(x)-f^{adc}f^{hbe}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x).\notag
\end{align}
\endgroup
The Group I member in common variables: first term $x\to z$, $z\to w$, $y\to x$; second term $x\to w$, $y\to x$; both give $\mathcal{E}(k\cdot(z-w))\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\big[\frac12\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)-\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}}\big]$ with the colour factor $[U(x')-U(w')]^{ab'}[N_{c}U^{ah}(x)-f^{adc}f^{hbe}U^{db}(z)U^{ce}(w)]$ (using $f^{adc}f^{hbe}U^{db}(w)U^{ce}(z)=f^{adc}f^{hbe}U^{db}(z)U^{ce}(w)$). Sum:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm C}}{d^{3}k}&={\color{pC}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,z,x'}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}\times\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg\{\Big[\Big(\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)-\frac{2}{(z-w)^{2}}\Big)-\Big(\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-2\,\mathcal{K}(w-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big)\Big]\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}+\Big(\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-2\,\mathcal{K}(w-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big)\Big[\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)-\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\Big]\bigg\}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{ah}(x)-f^{adc}f^{hbe}\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)}\label{eq:C-C}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Pair D}
\begingroup\color{pD}
\begin{align}
&\text{Group I Row III, part $\mathcal{E}$ of the third term of $N_{3b}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:C4}):}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\text{Group III Row VI, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-III6}, $\Psi$ term:}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\Big[U^{d'e}(x)-U^{d'e}(z)\Big]\rho^{d}(y)\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\overset{y'\to w',\,x\leftrightarrow y}{=}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y).\notag
\end{align}
\endgroup
Sum:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm D}}{d^{3}k}&={\color{pD}+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}\times\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg\{\Big[\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)-\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big]\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}+\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big[\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)-\Psi\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w')}\big)\Big]\bigg\}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)}\label{eq:C-D}
\end{align}
The kernels of the members in common variables were checked pointwise against the row forms (Sec.~\ref{sec:F}).

\subsection{The problematic third term of $N_{3b}$ (Group I Row III): tail only, after the $w$ integration}\label{sec:C4}
The third term of $N_{3b}$, contained in the last line of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3}, is
\begin{align}
&\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Big(-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\Big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&\quad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y).\notag
\end{align}
With $a_{t}=(x-w)-t(x-z)$, $-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}=-\int_{0}^{1}\frac{dt}{t}\big[1-\frac{(x-w)\cdot a_{t}}{a_{t}^{2}}\big]$: the LO kernel minus the LO kernel with the source shifted by the recoil; the soft integral is cut off at $t\simeq|x-w|/|z-w|$ without a phase, and nothing of this term is in the JIMWLK list. It is integrable at $z\to w$ ($\sim\log|z-w|/|z-w|$), but at large $z$ it behaves like $-\frac{1}{z^{2}}\log\frac{|z|}{|x-w|}$ times $\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\times$colour: isotropic, without the odd part of $\mathcal{E}$. It is split exactly as
\begin{align}
-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}={\color{pD}\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot(z-w)\big)}+{\color{cPr}\Big[-\frac12\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot(z-w)\big)\Big]}\,.\notag
\end{align}
{\color{pD}The first part is the Group I member of pair D} (its $\log|z|$ tail, for the $U(z)$-independent and the $U(z)$-linear colour parts alike, is cancelled by Group III Row VI, Eq.~\eqref{eq:C-D}). {\color{cPr}The second part is combined with the 24-term kernel of the same row (lines 1--4 of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3} without the third term of $N_{3b}$):}
\begingroup\color{cPr}
\begin{align}
\widehat{\mathcal{M}}&\equiv\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\quad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\quad+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag\\
&\quad-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)\notag\\
&\quad+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,.\label{eq:C-Mhat}
\end{align}
At fixed $x,y,x',w'$ and $|z|\to\infty$, with $\varphi\in(-\pi,\pi)$ the angle from $x-w$ to $z$, $e_{\perp}$ the unit vector $(x-w)/|x-w|$ rotated by $+\frac{\pi}{2}$, $\theta$ the angle between $k$ and $z$:
\begin{align}
\text{(24 terms)}&\;\longrightarrow\;\frac{\varphi}{z^{2}}\,\frac{e_{\perp}\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{|x-w|}+O\Big(\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{3}}\Big)\quad\text{(from the arccos terms; zero angular mean, not zero pointwise)},\notag\\
\text{(part $\ell-\mathcal{E}$)}&\;\longrightarrow\;\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}{z^{2}}\Big[\gamma_{E}+\log\big(|k|\,|x-w|\big)+\log|\cos\theta|+\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(\cos\theta)\Big]+\big(\text{terms}\propto e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)}\big)\,.\notag
\end{align}
Pointwise in $w$ the sum still has a $1/z^{2}$ tail. After the Fourier integration over $w$ ($r=x-w$, $e^{-ik\cdot w}=e^{-ik\cdot x}e^{ik\cdot r}$, convergence factor $e^{-\epsilon r}$), with $\chi(\theta)\equiv\log|2\cos\theta|+\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(\cos\theta)$,
\begin{align}
\int d^{2}r\,e^{ik\cdot r}\,\frac{r^{i}}{r^{2}}\Big[\gamma_{E}+\log(|k||r|)+\log|\cos\theta|+\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(\cos\theta)\Big]&=+\frac{2\pi i\,k^{i}}{|k|^{2}}\,\chi(\theta)\,,\qquad
\int d^{2}r\,e^{ik\cdot r}\,\varphi\,\frac{e_{\perp}^{i}}{|r|}=-\frac{2\pi i\,k^{i}}{|k|^{2}}\,\chi(\theta)\,:\notag
\end{align}
the two cancel, and
\begin{align}
\int d^{2}w\,e^{-ik\cdot w}\,\widehat{\mathcal{M}}=O\Big(\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{3}}\Big)+\big(\text{terms}\propto e^{-ik\cdot z}\big)\qquad(|z|\to\infty)\,,\notag
\end{align}
in every direction and for any $U(z)$ (the colour factor does not depend on $w$).
\endgroup
\noindent\textbf{Type: tail only, requires the $w$ integration before the $z$ integral} (paper Eqs.~(saw), (sawid), (Mhatclaim); fix\_soft Step 5: at $|z|=2560$ the two parts of $z^{2}\int_{w}e^{-ik\cdot w}\widehat{\mathcal{M}}$ are $6.933-2.690i$ and $-6.906+2.690i$, prediction $\pm(6.909-2.687i)$; $z^{2}\times$sum falls like $1/|z|$). The large-$z$ form of the 24-term kernel is established numerically; the Fourier identity is analytic. \emph{Answer to ``what cancels the problematic term'':} its $\log|z|$ part is cancelled by the soft remainder of Group III Row VI (pair D), its remaining $1/z^{2}$ part by the arccos (``odd-angle'') tail of the other 24 terms of its own row, after the $w$ integration.

\subsection{Cancellations inside single rows (no colour)}\label{sec:C5}
(i) Group I Row III: the poles on $(x-z)^{2}=(x-w)^{2}$ of the separate terms $N_{1},N_{2},N_{3a}$ (principal values, $\log|\cdot|$) cancel in the combined kernel (author's form; kernel value $0.19948358$ and $0.19948353$ at $\pm10^{-7}$ from the line, paper Sec.~5.3). (ii) Group II Row I, second part: poles on $(x'-w')^{2}(x-z)^{2}=(x-w)^{2}(x'-z)^{2}$ cancel in $N_{2a}+N_{2b}$ and $N_{2c}+N_{2d}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:A}, A6). (iii) Group III, instantaneous terms: the terms with $\mathcal{K}(x'-y')$ and $\mathcal{K}(x'-z)$ each grow like $\log\vee$ (BFKL term) and only their difference is finite. (iv) Group I Row II, $N_{2b}$: the non-integrable points $\mathbf{p}\to0$ and $\mathbf{q}\to\mathbf{p}$ of the author's kernel cancel in the antisymmetrised kernel. (v) Group I Row V, second part: the collinear point $z\to x$ of the regular term is integrable (angular integral first) by virtue of the $\frac{11}{6}$ term.

\subsection{The joint large-distance tail of Group II Row IV: tail only, requires joint integration}\label{sec:C6}
In the region in which the observed gluon, the unobserved gluon and their parent are all far from the projectile together ($w,w',z,y'\to\infty$ at fixed $w-z$, $w'-z$, $y'-z$; $z=Z\,n$, $n=(\cos\phi,\sin\phi)$, $\phi$ the angle between $k$ and $n$), three rows have $1/Z^{2}$ tails. With the far Wilson lines equal, the colour factors of the three rows are $+N_{c}\Gamma$ (Group II Row IV), $+N_{c}\Gamma$ (Group II Row I, all of the second part), $-N_{c}\Gamma$ (Group III Row III, second part), $\Gamma=[U(Y)-U(x')]^{ce'}[U(Y)-U(x)]^{ce}\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)$, and the three rows have the same tail density per $dt$ ($t=p^{+}/k^{+}$), $\varrho(t)=\frac{(t^{2}+t+1)^{2}+t^{2}\cos2\phi}{t\,(1+t)^{4}}$, with weights $+1,+1,-2$ (Group III with c.c.). In units
\begin{align}
\mathsf{u}\equiv2N_{c}\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{4\pi}{k^{2}}\int\frac{d^{2}Y}{Y^{2}}\int_{x,x'}\Big[U^{ce'}(Y)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(Y)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\notag
\end{align}
the tails of the finite terms as written are (fix\_tail, Steps 2--6):
\begin{center}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.3}\small
\begin{tabular}{|l|c|}\hline
{\color{cJt}Group II Row IV: DGLAP $+\frac{1}{12}$, RC $-\frac{11}{12}$, remnant Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-II4rem} $-1$, tensor term Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-II4} $+\frac16\cos2\phi$} & {\color{cJt}$-\frac{11}{6}+\frac16\cos2\phi$}\\
{\color{cJt}Group II Row I, second part, Eqs.~\eqref{eq:B-II2a}--\eqref{eq:B-II2e}} & {\color{cJt}$-\frac{11}{6}+\frac16\cos2\phi$}\\
{\color{cJt}Group III Row III, second part, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-III3two}, with c.c.} & {\color{cJt}$+\frac{11}{3}-\frac13\cos2\phi$}\\
Group II Row I, first part; Group III Row III, first part & $0$\\\hline
sum & $0$\\\hline
\end{tabular}
\end{center}
and the BFKL and JIMWLK coefficients of the three rows carry $1+1-2=0$ times $\mathsf{u}$ each. \textbf{Type: tail only, requires joint integration}: each row alone is logarithmically divergent at large pair position $Y$; the three rows have to be integrated together under one integral over the position of the pair (with $Y=\frac{y+y'}{2}$ of Row IV identified with $z$ of the other rows). This holds for any target uniform at large distance, and for a colour-neutral projectile for any target; for a coloured projectile and a target not uniform at large distance a remainder proportional to the total charge is left (the non-singlet term of the JIMWLK formula). Note that the remnant of Group II Row IV (missing in the author's file, \NOTE{} A5) is needed for this cancellation.
\subsection{The Coulomb logarithm of $\Delta N$ ($i\epsilon$ prescription): exact, against the complex conjugate}\label{sec:C7}
In the $i\epsilon$ prescription the term $\Delta N$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}, contains
\begingroup\color{gray}
\begin{align}
T_{\Lambda}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\pi\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,f^{abd}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\rho^{e}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\pi\log\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,f^{abd}\,\mathcal{A}^{a}(w')\,\bar{Q}^{d}\,\mathcal{A}^{b}(w)\,,\label{eq:C-Coul}
\end{align}
\endgroup
with $\mathcal{A}^{b}(w)\equiv\int_{y}\mathcal{K}(y-w)\big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\big]\rho^{c}(y)$ (the LO amplitude, a vector) and $\bar{Q}^{d}\equiv\int_{x}U^{de}(x)\rho^{e}(x)$ (the colour charge of the projectile after the collision): a forward (Coulomb) scattering phase of the observed gluon. Its complex conjugate is $\propto\int e^{+ik\cdot(w-w')}f^{abd}\mathcal{A}^{b}(w)\bar{Q}^{d}\mathcal{A}^{a}(w')$ (hermitian charges, real $U$); renaming $w\leftrightarrow w'$, $a\leftrightarrow b$ (which keeps the order of the charges: $\mathcal{A}(w')$, $\bar{Q}$, $\mathcal{A}(w)$) and $f^{bad}=-f^{abd}$ give $-T_{\Lambda}$. \textbf{Exact cancellation} of {\color{gray}$T_{\Lambda}+{\rm c.c.}=0$} (operator identity; checked with $SU(3)$ charges on three sites: $|T(w,w')^{\dagger}+T(w',w)|/|T|=9\times10^{-16}$, Sec.~\ref{sec:F}). It exists only in the $i\epsilon$ prescription; in the principal-value prescription there is no such term.

\section{What remains, with every three-$\rho$ product symmetrised: Group by Group, Row by Row}\label{sec:D}
\paragraph{What remains after the cancellations of Sec.~\ref{sec:C}.} (1) Group I Row IV and Group III Row I are removed (exact). (2) The $\frac{11}{3}$ terms of Group I Rows II and V are absent (exact). (3) All other terms of Sec.~\ref{sec:B} remain: Group I Rows II, III, V; Group II Rows I, IV; Group III Rows II, III, V, VI. Among them, the soft remainders of the pairs A--D (coloured as in Sec.~\ref{sec:C}) remain as terms of their rows; they are integrable only in the pair combinations Eqs.~\eqref{eq:C-A}--\eqref{eq:C-D}, i.e.\ each Group I member has to be integrated together with its Group III partner, under the renaming $y'\to w'$ (pair D: and $x\leftrightarrow y$). (4) The 24-term kernel of Group I Row III and the part $\ell-\mathcal{E}$ of the third term of $N_{3b}$ (blue) are integrable only with the $w$ integral done before the $z$ integral, Eq.~\eqref{eq:C-Mhat}. (5) Group II Row IV, Group II Row I (second part) and Group III Row III (second part) have to be integrated together over the position of the gluon pair (joint tail, Sec.~\ref{sec:C6}). (6) In the $i\epsilon$ prescription the additional term $\Delta N$ of Group I Row III, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}, remains, without its Coulomb logarithm (cancelled exactly against the complex conjugate, Sec.~\ref{sec:C7}); it is listed after Row III. Nothing is dropped in this section; only the order of the charges is rewritten.

\paragraph{Symmetrisation (exact, no average, no model).} With $\{\dots\}_{\rm sym}$ the average over all orderings of the operators inside the braces, $\{AB\}_{\rm sym}=\frac12(AB+BA)$, and $[\rho^{a}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=if^{abc}\rho^{c}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)$, every ordered product of three charges with a $c$-number tensor $C^{abc}$ is, for the three types that occur (paper draft Eqs.~(app\_symord)--(app\_syma12)),
\begin{align}
C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})&=\underbrace{C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}}_{\rm (i)}\notag\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}\Big[f^{abr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}+f^{bcr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{2}-p_{3})\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{r}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}}_{\rm (ii)}\notag\\
&\quad\underbrace{+\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\Big]}_{\rm (ii)}\notag\\
&\quad\underbrace{-\frac{1}{6}C^{abc}\big[f^{abr}f^{rcs}-f^{bcr}f^{ras}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,\rho^{s}(p_{1})}_{\rm (iii)}\,,\label{eq:D-ord}\\
C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\{\rho^{b}(p_{2}),\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}&=2C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+i\,C^{abc}\Big[f^{abr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad-\frac{1}{6}C^{abc}\big[f^{abr}f^{rcs}+f^{acr}f^{rbs}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,\rho^{s}(p_{1})\,,\label{eq:D-r12}\\
C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1}),\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}\rho^{c}(p_{3})&=2C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+i\,C^{abc}\Big[f^{bcr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{2}-p_{3})\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{r}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{6}C^{abc}\big[f^{bcr}f^{ras}+f^{acr}f^{rbs}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,\rho^{s}(p_{1})\,.\label{eq:D-r21}
\end{align}
(i) is the symmetrised three-$\rho$ term, (ii) the commutator (contact, two-$\rho$) terms, (iii) the double-commutator (one-$\rho$) terms. At a $\delta$-function the Wilson lines of the tensor are at the coincident point. For every row below: the colour tensor $C^{abc}$ (the indices $a,b,c$ belong to the charges in the order written, all other colour indices summed) is written out; equation (i) is the symmetrised three-$\rho$ term; equation (ii) contains the contact terms of the row's three-$\rho$ products together with the row's original two-$\rho$ terms; the one-$\rho$ terms (iii) have the prefactor, integrals, phase and kernels of (i), with $t\,C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}\rho^{b}\rho^{c}\}_{\rm sym}$ replaced by the last line of Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-ord}, \eqref{eq:D-r12} or \eqref{eq:D-r21} (they have zero average over a colour-invariant projectile). That each row's tensor, contracted with the charges in the row's order, is the colour operator of the row as written in Sec.~\ref{sec:B}, and that (i)+(ii)+(iii) equals it, was checked at the operator level (Sec.~\ref{sec:F}). The two-$\rho$ products of the original two-$\rho$ terms are kept ordered (if wanted: $\rho^{a}(p)\rho^{b}(q)=\{\rho^{a}(p)\rho^{b}(q)\}_{\rm sym}+\frac{i}{2}f^{abr}\delta^{(2)}(p-q)\rho^{r}(p)$).
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
four-$\rho$ row, evolution terms only; \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Colour tensor (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm I.II}}\rho^{a}(x')\big\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}$):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.II}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]\Big[f^{hbc}\,U^{gh}(w)-f^{gde}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ec}(y)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times 2C^{abc}_{{\rm I.II}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.II-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation and the row's original two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times i\,C^{abc}_{{\rm I.II}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.II-2}
\end{align}
and the original two-$\rho$ term of the row (unchanged):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\label{eq:D-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$ and the same with the other tensor):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(z)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,w}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(w)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{{\color{cPr}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}+\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{{\color{pA}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,w}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.III-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{{\color{cPr}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}+\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{{\color{pA}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,w}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III, $i\epsilon$ prescription ($\eta=+1$): the additional term $\Delta N$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$ and the same with the other tensor):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(z)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,w}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(w)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{-\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\pi\,\delta^{(2)}(z-w)\,\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,w}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.IIIdN-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,z}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{-\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\pi\,\delta^{(2)}(z-w)\,\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm I.III,w}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.IIIdN-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
cancels Group III Row I exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}); nothing remains
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Colour tensor (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm I.V}}\rho^{a}(x')\big\{\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{c}(y')\big\}$):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.V}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\Big[U^{dc}(x)\,U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\,U^{dc}(y')\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\mathcal{J}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\mathcal{E}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)-e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times 2C^{abc}_{{\rm I.V}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(y')\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.V-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation and the row's original two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\mathcal{J}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\mathcal{E}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)-e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times i\,C^{abc}_{{\rm I.V}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y')\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.V-2}
\end{align}
and the original two-$\rho$ terms of the row (second part, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I5two}; soft remainders of pair C in violet):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\,\frac{e^{-i\xi s}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&\qquad+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&\qquad-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}-\frac{\mathcal{E}(s)}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}-\frac{e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\,e^{-is}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\,,\label{eq:D-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1a}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\rho^{c}(x)$, and $C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1b}}\rho^{a}(x)\big\{\rho^{b}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}$):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1a}}&\equiv U^{ga}(w')\Big[f^{kbc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kb}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{ga}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\,U^{mb}(y)\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1b}}&\equiv U^{gb}(w)\Big[f^{kca}\,U^{gk}(w')-f^{gkl}\,U^{kc}(z)\,U^{la}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\,U^{mc}(y)\Big[f^{kha}\,U^{gk}(w')-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{la}(x)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times 2C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1a}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times 2C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1b}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]\label{eq:D-II.I-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation and the row's original two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times i\,C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1a}}\Big[f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times i\,C^{abc}_{{\rm II.I,1b}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:D-II.I-2}
\end{align}
and the original two-$\rho$ terms of the row (second part, unchanged; to be integrated together with Group II Row IV and Group III Row III, second part, Sec.~\ref{sec:C6}):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\qquad+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\qquad+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
All terms are two-$\rho$ terms (no symmetrisation needed): the DGLAP and RC terms of Secs.~3, 4 and
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\Big[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\label{eq:D-II.IV}\\
{\color{red}\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm rem}}{d^{3}k}}=&{\color{red}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[U^{ce'}(z)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)}\label{eq:D-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
cancels Group I Row IV exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}); nothing remains
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Colour tensor (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm III.II}}\rho^{a}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)$):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.II}}&\equiv U^{ma}(y)\,U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\Big[f^{kac}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{ka}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm III.II}}\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.II-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.II}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{r}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,y'}}\rho^{a}(x')\big\{\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}$ and the same with the other tensor):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,y'}}&\equiv f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,z}}&\equiv f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times 2C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,y'}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times 2C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,z}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.III-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation and the row's original two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times i\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,y'}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times i\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.III,z}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.III-2}
\end{align}
and the original two-$\rho$ terms of the row (second part, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-III3two}; soft remainder of pair C in violet):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,e^{-i\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}s}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pC}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,\frac{\Psi(s)}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,e^{-i\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}s}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag\\ &\qquad\times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right]\,,\label{eq:D-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,y'}}\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$ and the same with the other tensor):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,y'}}&\equiv f^{mkl}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,z}}&\equiv f^{mkl}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,y'}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,z}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.V-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,y'}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.V,z}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,y'}}\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{c}(x)$ and the same with the other tensor):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,y'}}&\equiv f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,z}}&\equiv f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
(i) Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (i)}_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,y'}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,z}}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
(ii) Two-$\rho$ terms: the contact terms of the symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{\rm (ii)}_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,y'}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{i}{2}\,C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,z}}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.VI-2}
\end{align}

\section{Average over the projectile: colour-singlet form, Group by Group, Row by Row}\label{sec:E}
\noindent\textbf{Author's choices (2026-11-20).} Global colour invariance of the projectile gives $\langle\rho^{a}(x)\rangle=0$ and
\begin{align}
\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=\big\langle\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\big\rangle_{x\neq y}=\delta^{ab}\,\frac{n(x,y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,,\qquad n(x,y)=n(y,x)\,;\notag
\end{align}
the symmetrised three-point function is of $d$-type,
\begin{align}
\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=d^{abc}\,\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,o(x,y,z)\,,\qquad o(x,y,z)\equiv\big\langle d^{efg}\{\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\rho^{g}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle\,,\notag
\end{align}
with the $f^{abc}$ part $g_{f}=0$; no model for $o$ ($o$ is real and totally symmetric; it is written with its arguments in the order of the charges); for $SU(2)$, $d^{abc}=0$ and the $o$ terms are absent. Hence, from Eqs.~\eqref{eq:D-ord}--\eqref{eq:D-r21} (the one-$\rho$ terms average to zero), with $C^{abc}$ the row's tensor,
\begin{align}
\langle C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\rangle&=\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,C^{abc}d^{abc}\,o(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})\notag\\
&\quad+\frac{i}{2(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\Big[\Phi_{12}\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,n(p_{1},p_{3})+\Phi_{23}\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{2}-p_{3})\,n(p_{1},p_{2})-\Phi_{13}\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,n(p_{1},p_{2})\Big],\notag
\end{align}
and for the anticommutator types the factor $2$ on the first term and $i[\Phi_{12}\delta_{12}n-\Phi_{13}\delta_{13}n]$, resp.\ $i[\Phi_{23}\delta_{23}n-\Phi_{13}\delta_{13}n]$; here $\Phi_{ij}=C^{abc}f^{abc}$ with the Wilson lines at $p_{i}$ and $p_{j}$ equal. Every colour factor is written below in colour-singlet form with traces of adjoint Wilson lines ($U^{\dagger}=U^{T}$, $(T^{e})_{bc}=-if^{ebc}$, $(D^{h})_{bc}=d^{hbc}$, ${\rm Tr}\,T^{a}T^{b}=N_{c}\delta^{ab}$):
\begin{align}
&{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)\}\,,\quad{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(b)U(c)\}=f^{ijk}f^{lmn}U^{il}(a)U^{jm}(b)U^{kn}(c)\,,\quad{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(c)U(d)\}\,,\notag\\
&{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(c)U(a)\}=i\,f^{ijk}d^{lmn}U^{il}(a)U^{jm}(b)U^{kn}(c)\,,\quad{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(c)U(e)\}\notag
\end{align}
(the traces with $D^{h}$ are purely imaginary, C-odd and vanish for $SU(2)$). In the fundamental representation ($U^{ab}=2{\rm tr}(t^{a}Vt^{b}V^{\dagger})$; fix\_dabc, exact):
\begin{align}
-i\,{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(c)U(a)\}&={\rm Im}\big[{\rm tr}(V^{\dagger}_{a}V_{b})\,{\rm tr}(V^{\dagger}_{b}V_{c})\,{\rm tr}(V^{\dagger}_{c}V_{a})\big]+{\rm Im}\,{\rm tr}\big(V^{\dagger}_{a}V_{b}V^{\dagger}_{c}V_{a}V^{\dagger}_{b}V_{c}\big)\,,\notag\\
i\,{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(c)U(e)\}&=-{\rm Im}\big[{\rm tr}A\,{\rm tr}B\,{\rm tr}(A^{\dagger}B^{\dagger})\big]-{\rm Im}\,{\rm tr}\big(ABA^{\dagger}B^{\dagger}\big)\,,\qquad A=V^{\dagger}_{a}V_{b}\,,\ B=V^{\dagger}_{c}V_{e}\,.\notag
\end{align}
Each row below has the prefactor, integrals, phase and kernels of the same row in Sec.~\ref{sec:D}; only the colour operator is averaged. The $(3\rho)$ equations are the part of the finite terms odd in $k$ (configuration by configuration; they change sign with the phase convention, Sec.~\ref{sec:conv}), the $(2\rho)$ equations the even part; for the additional term $\Delta N$ of the $i\epsilon$ prescription it is the reverse (its $(3\rho)$ part is even, its contact part odd in $k$). The singlet colour factors of $\Delta N$ are those of Group I Row III (same colour tensors), with the kernels of $\Delta N$. These are the row equations of the paper draft (Eqs.~(sIIa)--(oIIIVI)) in the notation of this file, with the colour factors written as traces.
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
four-$\rho$ row, evolution terms only; \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{{\color{cPr}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
