&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}+\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{{\color{pA}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.III-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{{\color{cPr}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{cPr}+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}+\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{{\color{pA}-\mathcal{E}\big(k\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\big)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III, $i\epsilon$ prescription ($\eta=+1$): the additional term $\Delta N$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{-\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\pi\,\delta^{(2)}(z-w)\,\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.IIIdN-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{-\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\pi\,\delta^{(2)}(z-w)\,\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.IIIdN-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
cancels Group III Row I exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}); nothing remains
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\mathcal{J}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\mathcal{E}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)-e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,o(x',y,y')\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(y)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.V-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\mathcal{J}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\mathcal{E}(s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)-e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,y')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\,n(y',y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.V-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ terms (second part):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\,\frac{e^{-i\xi s}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&\qquad+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&\qquad-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}-\frac{\mathcal{E}(s)}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}-\frac{e^{-is}\,\mathcal{E}(-s)}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\ &\qquad\qquad-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+N_{c}\Big(-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\,e^{-is}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:E-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x,x',y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w')\}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:E-II.I-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]\label{eq:E-II.I-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ terms (second part):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\quad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &\qquad-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\quad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &\qquad-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\qquad+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\quad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &\qquad-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\qquad+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\quad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &\qquad-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\quad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &\qquad-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
All terms are quadratic; the DGLAP, RC (Secs.~3, 4) and LO+RC terms in singlet form follow by the replacement $[U^{ce'}(y')-U^{ce'}(x')][U^{ce}(y)-U^{ce}(x)]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\to\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}$; the finite terms are
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\label{eq:E-II.IV}\\
{\color{red}\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm rem}}{d^{3}k}}=&{\color{red}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)\Big\}}\label{eq:E-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
cancels Group I Row IV exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}); nothing remains
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(y,x',x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(y')\}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.III-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pB}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ terms (second part):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,e^{-i\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}s}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\quad\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&\qquad+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &\qquad\qquad+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pC}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,\frac{\Psi(s)}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pC}\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &\qquad\qquad+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\,e^{-i\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}s}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\qquad\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &\qquad\qquad+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\ &\qquad\qquad-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\ &\qquad\qquad-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:E-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.V-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pA}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\quad\times\bigg\{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad{\color{pD}-\Psi(s)\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&\qquad-\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&\quad+\bigg\{+\Big[\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}\Big]\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\qquad\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\quad \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
&\qquad\quad -2{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.VI-2}
\end{align}

\section{Checks}\label{sec:F}
\sloppy
\noindent Scripts (numpy/scipy, run with \texttt{python -I}) and logs in \texttt{scratchpad/final\_explicit/}: \texttt{phases.py}, \texttt{s2\_kernels.py}, \texttt{s3\_colour.py}, \texttt{s4\_operator.py}, \texttt{s5\_singlet.py}, \texttt{s6\_pairs.py}; the TeX of Secs.~\ref{sec:B}--\ref{sec:E} is generated by \texttt{partB.py}, \texttt{partC.py}, \texttt{partDE.py} from the same data that the checks use (colour brackets of Sec.~\ref{sec:E}: \texttt{singlet\_data.py}). Random $SU(3)$ (and $SU(4)$) Wilson lines; deviations are maximal relative deviations.
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Phase.} Inventory of the author's file: 57 phases, all $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{primed}-\text{unprimed})}$ (uniform). All phases of this file outside the author's text after \verb|\end{document}| are $e^{-ik\cdot(\text{unprimed}-\text{primed})}$ (red), except where the author's convention is quoted in Sec.~\ref{sec:A}.
\item \textbf{Exact cancellations (a).} Group I Row IV $+$ Group III Row I: colour tensors equal to $1.6\times10^{-15}$ ($SU(3)$), $2.4\times10^{-15}$ ($SU(4)$); kernels identical term by term. $\frac{11}{3}$ terms: relabelled colour factor of Row V equal to minus that of Row II exactly ($0$); prefactors $\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{4}}\frac{11}{6}=\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\frac{11}{3}$.
\item \textbf{Colour identities of the pairs} (Eq.~\eqref{eq:C-colid}): A $0$, B $0$, C $4.9\times10^{-15}$, D $0$ ($SU(3)$, $SU(4)$); antisymmetry of both colour structures of Row I.II $N_{2b}$ under $x\leftrightarrow y$: $2.2\times10^{-16}$. Pairs B and C: the two Group I Row V recoil terms, renamed to the common variables, equal the common forms used in Eqs.~\eqref{eq:C-B}, \eqref{eq:C-C}: $2.5\times10^{-16}$, $8.8\times10^{-16}$ (kernel $\times$ colour, 20 random configurations).
\item \textbf{Equivalence of the author's forms} (Sec.~\ref{sec:A}): Group I Row III combined kernel vs verified $\mathcal{M}^{ij}+\ell\,K\,\mathcal{K}\cdot\mathcal{K}$: $4.1\times10^{-13}$ (200 points); Group I Row V second part, plus prescriptions vs regular $+$ soft form (quadrature): $4.0\times10^{-13}$; Group III Row III second part, integrand identity: $5.8\times10^{-14}$; Group II Row I second part, author's $N_{2a}+\dots+N_{2e}$ vs verified $\mathfrak{K}_{2a}+\dots+\mathfrak{K}_{2f}$: $8.7\times10^{-13}$, and near the surface $(x'-w')^{2}(x-z)^{2}=(x-w)^{2}(x'-z)^{2}$ at relative distance $10^{-5}$: $N_{2a}$ alone $\pm3.6\times10^{3}$, $N_{2a}+N_{2b}$ $0.0951/0.0951$, total $-0.47438/-0.47438$; Group II Row IV $p^{+}\to\xi$: $4\times10^{-16}$; $\int_{0}^{1}[\xi(1-\xi)-2]d\xi=-\frac{11}{6}$; closed forms of $\mathcal{E},\Psi,\widehat{\mathcal{E}}$ vs quadrature $5\times10^{-11}$; cut $\vee$: $|\int_{0}^{T}\frac{e^{-ist}}{1+t}dt-\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(s)|=5.3\times10^{-2},5.8\times10^{-3},5.9\times10^{-4},5.9\times10^{-5}$ for $T=10,\dots,10^{4}$.
\item \textbf{Last term of $N_{3b}$ (NOTE A2).} At $z=R(\cos0.7,\sin0.7)$, units $\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}$, colour stripped: $z^{2}\times$author's term $=-16.06,-23.97,-31.82$, $z^{2}\times$verified term $=16.60+5.31i,\ 24.55+5.34i,\ 32.39+5.34i$ for $R=10^{2},10^{3},10^{4}$; with the Group III Row V partner: $|z^{2}\times(\text{verified}+\text{III.V})|=6.7\times10^{-2},1.5\times10^{-2},1.4\times10^{-3}$, $z^{2}\times(\text{author}+\text{III.V})=-32.6,-48.5,-64.2$ (real parts). Pointwise $|\ell+\mathcal{E}|=0.78,2.28,0.39,1.49,1.37$ at five random points.
\item \textbf{Symmetrisation at the operator level (b).} SU(3) fundamental charges as explicit $3\times3$ generators on three sites ($27$-dimensional space), $\delta$-functions as Kronecker deltas of the sites, random Wilson lines (equal at coinciding sites, and independent): for all $27$ assignments of the three charge points of each of the thirteen cubic colour structures to the sites ($702$ cases), the colour operator of the row as written in Sec.~\ref{sec:B} equals the tensor $C^{abc}$ of Sec.~\ref{sec:D} contracted with the ordered charges, and equals (i)+(ii)+(iii): maximal deviation $2.0\times10^{-15}$ (relative; absolute for operators of norm below one, e.g.~Row I.II at $x=y$, where the operator vanishes). Group III Row VI: author's simplified colour operator equals the earlier form with $U(z)U(z)$: $1.2\times10^{-15}$. Control: omitting the contact terms at $x'=x$ gives a relative deviation $0.78$.
\item \textbf{Colour-singlet forms (c).} Trace forms: ${\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(b)U(c)\}=f^{ijk}f^{lmn}U^{il}(a)U^{jm}(b)U^{kn}(c)$ and $-i{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(a)U(b)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(c)U(a)\}=f^{ijk}d^{lmn}U^{il}(a)U^{jm}(b)U^{kn}(c)$ (real): $1.1\times10^{-15}$; fundamental forms: $1.0\times10^{-15}$. Thirteen cubic structures: printed contact coefficients $=$ row factor $\times$ generic factor exactly; contact brackets (traces) vs $C^{abc}f^{abc}$ at the coincident points: $2.4\times10^{-15}$ ($SU(3)$), $5.4\times10^{-15}$ ($SU(4)$); $(3\rho)$ lines as printed (coefficient $\times$ traces with $D^{h}$) vs $t\,C^{abc}d^{abc}$: $1.9\times10^{-15}$, $3.7\times10^{-15}$; quadratic rows (I.II, I.V, II.I, III.III, pair C) vs $C^{aa}$: $\le2.6\times10^{-15}$. The brackets are those of the paper draft (Eqs.~(sIIa)--(oIIIVI)), converted to traces; the conversion is part of the check.
\item \textbf{$i\epsilon$ prescription ($\Delta N$).} The bracket multiplying $\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}$ in Group I Row III equals the $\cos2\phi$/$\sin2\phi$ form, Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-Bij}: $2.9\times10^{-13}$ (500 points); at $z\to w$ its angular mean times $2(z-w)^{2}$ tends to $\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)$ ($0.57025701$ against $0.57025702$ at $|z-w|=10^{-4}$); the Coulomb term: $|T(w,w')^{\dagger}+T(w',w)|/|T|=9.2\times10^{-16}$ at the operator level ($SU(3)$ charges on three sites), i.e.\ it cancels against its complex conjugate (\texttt{s7\_ieps.py}). The exact combination of the $i\pi\theta$ terms into $-i\eta\pi$ (sympy), the residues against a Lorentzian-regulated $p^{+}$ integral and the window integral $2\pi\log\frac{\rho_{0}k^{+}}{\Lambda|x-w|}$ are from \texttt{paper/notes/scripts/ref3\_presc/} (a2--a5), not recomputed.
\item \textbf{Tail cancellations} (not recomputed here; from the fix notes): pairs A--D, $z^{2}\times$sum falls like $1/|z|$ in every direction (fix\_soft Step 3); problematic term, $z^{2}\int_{w}$ of the two parts $6.933-2.690i$ and $-6.906+2.690i$ at $|z|=2560$ (fix\_soft Step 5); joint tail $1:1:-2$ (fix\_tail, analytic and 60-digit numerics).
\item \textbf{Not done.} No complete numerical integration of the finite terms over all transverse positions with Wilson lines (as in the verified files). The integrability of the contact kernels at the new coincidences of the merged charge point with $z,w,w',y'$ has been checked only for two kernels (paper draft, remark (ii) of Sec.~5.7).
\end{itemize}

\end{document}
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N^{3\rho}_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(z-w)^{i}(x-z)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w)^{i}(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}(x-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w)^{i}(x-z)^{j}}{(x-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\tfrac12U^{ab'}(x')U^{gb}(z)U^{ge}(y)\big[\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big]\big[f^{cbd}U^{ac}(w)\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}U^{cb}(z)U^{dm}(x)\rho^{m}(x)\big]\notag\\
&+\Big(\tfrac12U^{ab'}(w')-U^{ab'}(x')\Big)\big[\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big]\big[f^{cbd}U^{ac}(w)\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}U^{cb}(z)U^{dm}(x)\rho^{m}(x)\big]\notag\\
&-\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,f^{abd}U^{bc}(z)U^{de}(x)\big[\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big]\notag\\
&+\tfrac12\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big(f^{hbc}U^{ah}(w)\big[\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\big]+f^{abc}U^{bd}(x)U^{ce}(y)\big[\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\big]\Big)\bigg\}\notag\\[6pt]
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\frac{\mathrm{arc\,cos}\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}-[(x-w)\cdot(x-z)]^{2}}}\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[{(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}{-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,(y-z)\cdot(z-w)}{-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,(y-z)\cdot(z-w)}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\Big[{\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,(y-z)\cdot(z-w)}{(x-w)^{2}}}{-\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}}\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\tfrac12\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,f^{adc}U^{db}(x)\big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(y)\big]\big[\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big]\notag\\[6pt]
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\int_{x,y,z,x',w',r}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-r)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)\,(x-r)\cdot(z-r)}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}(x-r)^{2}(z-r)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big\{f^{abd}U^{de}(x)\big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)-\tfrac12f^{adc}U^{db}(x)\big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(y)\big]\big[\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big]\Big\}\notag\\[6pt]
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-w)\,(x-z)\cdot(w-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}(w-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\big[U^{de}(x)U^{bc}(w)-U^{de}(x)U^{bc}(y)\big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\[6pt]
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(z-w)^{i}(x-z)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w)^{i}(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}(x-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w)^{i}(x-z)^{j}}{(x-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&\times\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\big[U^{de}(x)U^{bc}(z)-U^{de}(x)U^{bc}(y)\big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\[6pt]
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,x',y',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg(\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(z-x)\,(y-z)\cdot(y'-x)}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}(y-z)^{2}(y'-x)^{2}}-2\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)\,(z-x)\cdot(y'-x)}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}(y-z)^{2}(y'-x)^{2}}\bigg)\notag\\
&\times\big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,f^{adc}\big[U^{db}(x)-U^{db}(y')\big]\big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(y)\big]\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(y')\}\notag\\[6pt]
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,e^{-ik\cdot\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}(y'-z)}\frac{(y'-z)^{m}\,(y-w)^{i}\,(x-z)^{j}}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\delta_{k'm}\delta_{ij}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}-\Big(\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big)\delta_{jm}\delta_{ik'}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}\bigg]\times\frac{(x'-y')^{k'}}{(x'-y')^{2}}\,f^{c'd'a}\big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}\frac{(x'-z)^{k'}}{(x'-z)^{2}}\,f^{c'd'a}\big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Bigg\}\times\bigg\{\big[U^{d'b}(z)-U^{d'b}(x)\big]\rho^{b}(x)\,\big[U^{ad}(w)-U^{ad}(y)\big]\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&+\Big[\tfrac12U^{d'b}(z)U^{ad}(w)-U^{d'b}(z)U^{ad}(y)+\tfrac12U^{d'b}(x)U^{ad}(y)\Big]\big[\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big]\bigg\}+{\rm c.c.}
\end{align}
